1→#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2→"""
     3→Hermes CLI - Main entry point.
     4→
     5→Usage:
     6→    hermes                     # Interactive chat (default)
     7→    hermes chat                # Interactive chat
     8→    hermes gateway             # Run gateway in foreground
     9→    hermes gateway start       # Start gateway as service
    10→    hermes gateway stop        # Stop gateway service
    11→    hermes gateway status      # Show gateway status
    12→    hermes gateway install     # Install gateway service
    13→    hermes gateway uninstall   # Uninstall gateway service
    14→    hermes setup               # Interactive setup wizard
    15→    hermes logout              # Clear stored authentication
    16→    hermes status              # Show status of all components
    17→    hermes cron                # Manage cron jobs
    18→    hermes cron list           # List cron jobs
    19→    hermes cron status         # Check if cron scheduler is running
    20→    hermes doctor              # Check configuration and dependencies
    21→    hermes honcho setup                    # Configure Honcho AI memory integration
    22→    hermes honcho status                   # Show Honcho config and connection status
    23→    hermes honcho sessions                 # List directory → session name mappings
    24→    hermes honcho map <name>               # Map current directory to a session name
    25→    hermes honcho peer                     # Show peer names and dialectic settings
    26→    hermes honcho peer --user NAME         # Set user peer name
    27→    hermes honcho peer --ai NAME           # Set AI peer name
    28→    hermes honcho peer --reasoning LEVEL   # Set dialectic reasoning level
    29→    hermes honcho mode                     # Show current memory mode
    30→    hermes honcho mode [hybrid|honcho|local]  # Set memory mode
    31→    hermes honcho tokens                   # Show token budget settings
    32→    hermes honcho tokens --context N       # Set session.context() token cap
    33→    hermes honcho tokens --dialectic N     # Set dialectic result char cap
    34→    hermes honcho identity                 # Show AI peer identity representation
    35→    hermes honcho identity <file>          # Seed AI peer identity from a file (SOUL.md etc.)
    36→    hermes honcho migrate                  # Step-by-step migration guide: OpenClaw native → Hermes + Honcho
    37→    hermes version             # Show version
    38→    hermes update              # Update to latest version
    39→    hermes uninstall           # Uninstall Hermes Agent
    40→    hermes sessions browse     # Interactive session picker with search
    41→    hermes claw migrate        # Migrate from OpenClaw to Hermes
    42→    hermes claw migrate --dry-run  # Preview migration without changes

    25→def _get_skin():

    34→def get_skin_faces(key: str, default: list) -> list:

    37→    if skin:

    39→        if faces:
    40→            return faces
    41→    return default
    42→
    43→
    44→def get_skin_verbs() -> list:
    45→    """Get thinking verbs from active skin."""

    49→        if verbs:

    62→# =========================================================================

    66→def _oneline(text: str) -> str:

    68→    return " ".join(text.split())
    69→
    70→
    71→def build_tool_preview(tool_name: str, args: dict, max_len: int = 40) -> str:
    72→    """Build a short preview of a tool call's primary argument for display."""
    73→    if not args:
    74→        return None

    82→        "skill_view": "name", "skills_list": "category",
    83→        "schedule_cronjob": "name",
    84→        "execute_code": "code", "delegate_task": "goal",
    85→        "clarify": "question", "skill_manage": "name",

    98→        if timeout_val and action == "wait":
    99→            parts.append(f"{timeout_val}s")
   100→        return " ".join(parts) if parts else None
   101→

   103→        todos_arg = args.get("todos")
   104→        merge = args.get("merge", False)

   193→        "♪(´ε` )", "(◕ᴗ◕✿)", "ヾ(＾∇＾)", "(≧◡≦)", "(★ω★)",
   194→    ]
   195→
   196→    KAWAII_THINKING = [
   197→        "(｡•́︿•̀｡)", "(◔_◔)", "(¬‿¬)", "( •_•)>⌐■-■", "(⌐■_■)",
   198→        "(´･_･`)", "◉_◉", "(°ロ°)", "( ˘⌣˘)♡", "ヽ(>∀<☆)☆",
   199→        "٩(๑❛ᴗ❛๑)۶", "(⊙_⊙)", "(¬_¬)", "( ͡° ͜ʖ ͡°)", "ಠ_ಠ",
   200→    ]
   201→
   202→    THINKING_VERBS = [
   203→        "pondering", "contemplating", "musing", "cogitating", "ruminating",
   204→        "deliberating", "mulling", "reflecting", "processing", "reasoning",
   205→        "analyzing", "computing", "synthesizing", "formulating", "brainstorming",
   206→    ]
   207→
   208→    def __init__(self, message: str = "", spinner_type: str = 'dots'):
   209→        self.message = message
   210→        self.spinner_frames = self.SPINNERS.get(spinner_type, self.SPINNERS['dots'])
   211→        self.running = False
   212→        self.thread = None
   213→        self.frame_idx = 0
   214→        self.start_time = None
   215→        self.last_line_len = 0
   216→        self._last_flush_time = 0.0  # Rate-limit flushes for patch_stdout compat
   217→        # Capture stdout NOW, before any redirect_stdout(devnull) from
   218→        # child agents can replace sys.stdout with a black hole.
   219→        self._out = sys.stdout
   220→
   221→    def _write(self, text: str, end: str = '\n', flush: bool = False):
   222→        """Write to the stdout captured at spinner creation time."""
   223→        try:
   224→            self._out.write(text + end)
   225→            if flush:
   226→                self._out.flush()
   227→        except (ValueError, OSError):
   228→            pass
   229→
   230→    def _animate(self):
   231→        # Cache skin wings at start (avoid per-frame imports)
   232→        skin = _get_skin()
   233→        wings = skin.get_spinner_wings() if skin else []
   234→
   235→        while self.running:
   236→            if os.getenv("HERMES_SPINNER_PAUSE"):
   237→                time.sleep(0.1)
   238→                continue
   239→            frame = self.spinner_frames[self.frame_idx % len(self.spinner_frames)]
   240→            elapsed = time.time() - self.start_time
   241→            if wings:
   242→                left, right = wings[self.frame_idx % len(wings)]
   243→                line = f"  {left} {frame} {self.message} {right} ({elapsed:.1f}s)"
   244→            else:
   245→                line = f"  {frame} {self.message} ({elapsed:.1f}s)"
   246→            pad = max(self.last_line_len - len(line), 0)
   247→            # Rate-limit flush() calls to avoid spinner spam under
   248→            # prompt_toolkit's patch_stdout.  Each flush() pushes a queue
   249→            # item that may trigger a separate run_in_terminal() call; if
   250→            # items are processed one-at-a-time the \r overwrite is lost
   251→            # and every frame appears on its own line.  By flushing at
   252→            # most every 0.4s we guarantee multiple \r-frames are batched
   253→            # into a single write, so the terminal collapses them correctly.
   254→            now = time.time()
   255→            should_flush = (now - self._last_flush_time) >= 0.4
   256→            self._write(f"\r{line}{' ' * pad}", end='', flush=should_flush)
   257→            if should_flush:
   258→                self._last_flush_time = now
   259→            self.last_line_len = len(line)
   260→            self.frame_idx += 1
   261→            time.sleep(0.12)
   262→
   263→    def start(self):
   264→        if self.running:
   265→            return
   266→        self.running = True
   267→        self.start_time = time.time()
   268→        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self._animate, daemon=True)
   269→        self.thread.start()
   270→
   271→    def update_text(self, new_message: str):
   272→        self.message = new_message
   273→
   274→    def print_above(self, text: str):
   275→        """Print a line above the spinner without disrupting animation.
   276→
   277→        Clears the current spinner line, prints the text, and lets the
   278→        next animation tick redraw the spinner on the line below.
   279→        Thread-safe: uses the captured stdout reference (self._out).
   280→        Works inside redirect_stdout(devnull) because _write bypasses
   281→        sys.stdout and writes to the stdout captured at spinner creation.
   282→        """
   283→        if not self.running:
   284→            self._write(f"  {text}", flush=True)
   285→            return
   286→        # Clear spinner line with spaces (not \033[K) to avoid garbled escape
   287→        # codes when prompt_toolkit's patch_stdout is active — same approach
   288→        # as stop(). Then print text; spinner redraws on next tick.
   289→        blanks = ' ' * max(self.last_line_len + 5, 40)
   290→        self._write(f"\r{blanks}\r  {text}", flush=True)
   291→
   292→    def stop(self, final_message: str = None):
   293→        self.running = False
   294→        if self.thread:
   295→            self.thread.join(timeout=0.5)
   296→        # Clear the spinner line with spaces instead of \033[K to avoid
   297→        # garbled escape codes when prompt_toolkit's patch_stdout is active.
   298→        blanks = ' ' * max(self.last_line_len + 5, 40)
   299→        self._write(f"\r{blanks}\r", end='', flush=True)
   300→        if final_message:
   301→            self._write(f"  {final_message}", flush=True)
   302→
   303→    def __enter__(self):
   304→        self.start()
   305→        return self
   306→
   307→    def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc_val, exc_tb):
   308→        self.stop()
   309→        return False
   310→

   352→# =========================================================================
   353→# Cute tool message (completion line that replaces the spinner)
   354→# =========================================================================
   355→
   356→def _detect_tool_failure(tool_name: str, result: str | None) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   357→    """Inspect a tool result string for signs of failure.
   358→
   359→    Returns ``(is_failure, suffix)`` where *suffix* is an informational tag
   360→    like ``" [exit 1]"`` for terminal failures, or ``" [error]"`` for generic
   361→    failures.  On success, returns ``(False, "")``.
   362→    """
   363→    if result is None:
   364→        return False, ""
   365→
   366→    if tool_name == "terminal":
   367→        try:
   368→            data = json.loads(result)
   369→            exit_code = data.get("exit_code")
   370→            if exit_code is not None and exit_code != 0:
   371→                return True, f" [exit {exit_code}]"
   372→        except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError, AttributeError):
   373→            logger.debug("Could not parse terminal result as JSON for exit code check")
   374→        return False, ""
   375→
   376→    # Memory-specific: distinguish "full" from real errors
   377→    if tool_name == "memory":
   378→        try:
   379→            data = json.loads(result)
   380→            if data.get("success") is False and "exceed the limit" in data.get("error", ""):
   381→                return True, " [full]"
   382→        except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError, AttributeError):
   383→            logger.debug("Could not parse memory result as JSON for capacity check")
   384→
   385→    # Generic heuristic for non-terminal tools
   386→    lower = result[:500].lower()
   387→    if '"error"' in lower or '"failed"' in lower or result.startswith("Error"):
   388→        return True, " [error]"
   389→
   390→    return False, ""
   391→
   392→
   393→def get_cute_tool_message(
   394→    tool_name: str, args: dict, duration: float, result: str | None = None,
   395→) -> str:
   396→    """Generate a formatted tool completion line for CLI quiet mode.
   397→
   398→    Format: ``| {emoji} {verb:9} {detail}  {duration}``
   399→
   400→    When *result* is provided the line is checked for failure indicators.
   401→    Failed tool calls get a red prefix and an informational suffix.
   402→    """
   403→    dur = f"{duration:.1f}s"
   404→    is_failure, failure_suffix = _detect_tool_failure(tool_name, result)
   405→    skin_prefix = get_skin_tool_prefix()
   406→
   407→    def _trunc(s, n=40):
   408→        s = str(s)
   409→        return (s[:n-3] + "...") if len(s) > n else s
   410→
   411→    def _path(p, n=35):
   412→        p = str(p)
   413→        return ("..." + p[-(n-3):]) if len(p) > n else p
   414→
   415→    def _wrap(line: str) -> str:
   416→        """Apply skin tool prefix and failure suffix."""
   417→        if skin_prefix != "┊":
   418→            line = line.replace("┊", skin_prefix, 1)
   419→        if not is_failure:
   420→            return line
   421→        return f"{line}{failure_suffix}"
   422→
   423→    if tool_name == "web_search":
   424→        return _wrap(f"┊ 🔍 search    {_trunc(args.get('query', ''), 42)}  {dur}")
   425→    if tool_name == "web_extract":
   426→        urls = args.get("urls", [])
   427→        if urls:
   428→            url = urls[0] if isinstance(urls, list) else str(urls)
   429→            domain = url.replace("https://", "").replace("http://", "").split("/")[0]
   430→            extra = f" +{len(urls)-1}" if len(urls) > 1 else ""
   431→            return _wrap(f"┊ 📄 fetch     {_trunc(domain, 35)}{extra}  {dur}")
   432→        return _wrap(f"┊ 📄 fetch     pages  {dur}")
   433→    if tool_name == "web_crawl":
   434→        url = args.get("url", "")
   435→        domain = url.replace("https://", "").replace("http://", "").split("/")[0]
   436→        return _wrap(f"┊ 🕸️  crawl     {_trunc(domain, 35)}  {dur}")
   437→    if tool_name == "terminal":
   438→        return _wrap(f"┊ 💻 $         {_trunc(args.get('command', ''), 42)}  {dur}")
   439→    if tool_name == "process":
   440→        action = args.get("action", "?")
   441→        sid = args.get("session_id", "")[:12]
   442→        labels = {"list": "ls processes", "poll": f"poll {sid}", "log": f"log {sid}",
   443→                  "wait": f"wait {sid}", "kill": f"kill {sid}", "write": f"write {sid}", "submit": f"submit {sid}"}
   444→        return _wrap(f"┊ ⚙️  proc      {labels.get(action, f'{action} {sid}')}  {dur}")
   445→    if tool_name == "read_file":
   446→        return _wrap(f"┊ 📖 read      {_path(args.get('path', ''))}  {dur}")
   447→    if tool_name == "write_file":
   448→        return _wrap(f"┊ ✍️  write     {_path(args.get('path', ''))}  {dur}")
   449→    if tool_name == "patch":
   450→        return _wrap(f"┊ 🔧 patch     {_path(args.get('path', ''))}  {dur}")
   451→    if tool_name == "search_files":
   452→        pattern = _trunc(args.get("pattern", ""), 35)
   453→        target = args.get("target", "content")
   454→        verb = "find" if target == "files" else "grep"
   455→        return _wrap(f"┊ 🔎 {verb:9} {pattern}  {dur}")
   456→    if tool_name == "browser_navigate":
   457→        url = args.get("url", "")
   458→        domain = url.replace("https://", "").replace("http://", "").split("/")[0]
   459→        return _wrap(f"┊ 🌐 navigate  {_trunc(domain, 35)}  {dur}")
   460→    if tool_name == "browser_snapshot":
   461→        mode = "full" if args.get("full") else "compact"
   462→        return _wrap(f"┊ 📸 snapshot  {mode}  {dur}")
   463→    if tool_name == "browser_click":
   464→        return _wrap(f"┊ 👆 click     {args.get('ref', '?')}  {dur}")
   465→    if tool_name == "browser_type":
   466→        return _wrap(f"┊ ⌨️  type      \"{_trunc(args.get('text', ''), 30)}\"  {dur}")
   467→    if tool_name == "browser_scroll":
   468→        d = args.get("direction", "down")
   469→        arrow = {"down": "↓", "up": "↑", "right": "→", "left": "←"}.get(d, "↓")
   470→        return _wrap(f"┊ {arrow}  scroll    {d}  {dur}")
   471→    if tool_name == "browser_back":
   472→        return _wrap(f"┊ ◀️  back      {dur}")
   473→    if tool_name == "browser_press":
   474→        return _wrap(f"┊ ⌨️  press     {args.get('key', '?')}  {dur}")
   475→    if tool_name == "browser_close":
   476→        return _wrap(f"┊ 🚪 close     browser  {dur}")
   477→    if tool_name == "browser_get_images":
   478→        return _wrap(f"┊ 🖼️  images    extracting  {dur}")
   479→    if tool_name == "browser_vision":
   480→        return _wrap(f"┊ 👁️  vision    analyzing page  {dur}")
   481→    if tool_name == "todo":
   482→        todos_arg = args.get("todos")
   483→        merge = args.get("merge", False)
   484→        if todos_arg is None:
   485→            return _wrap(f"┊ 📋 plan      reading tasks  {dur}")
   486→        elif merge:
   487→            return _wrap(f"┊ 📋 plan      update {len(todos_arg)} task(s)  {dur}")
   488→        else:
   489→            return _wrap(f"┊ 📋 plan      {len(todos_arg)} task(s)  {dur}")
   490→    if tool_name == "session_search":
   491→        return _wrap(f"┊ 🔍 recall    \"{_trunc(args.get('query', ''), 35)}\"  {dur}")
   492→    if tool_name == "memory":
   493→        action = args.get("action", "?")
   494→        target = args.get("target", "")
   495→        if action == "add":
   496→            return _wrap(f"┊ 🧠 memory    +{target}: \"{_trunc(args.get('content', ''), 30)}\"  {dur}")
   497→        elif action == "replace":
   498→            return _wrap(f"┊ 🧠 memory    ~{target}: \"{_trunc(args.get('old_text', ''), 20)}\"  {dur}")
   499→        elif action == "remove":
   500→            return _wrap(f"┊ 🧠 memory    -{target}: \"{_trunc(args.get('old_text', ''), 20)}\"  {dur}")
   501→        return _wrap(f"┊ 🧠 memory    {action}  {dur}")
   502→    if tool_name == "skills_list":
   503→        return _wrap(f"┊ 📚 skills    list {args.get('category', 'all')}  {dur}")
   504→    if tool_name == "skill_view":
   505→        return _wrap(f"┊ 📚 skill     {_trunc(args.get('name', ''), 30)}  {dur}")
   506→    if tool_name == "image_generate":
   507→        return _wrap(f"┊ 🎨 create    {_trunc(args.get('prompt', ''), 35)}  {dur}")
   508→    if tool_name == "text_to_speech":
   509→        return _wrap(f"┊ 🔊 speak     {_trunc(args.get('text', ''), 30)}  {dur}")
   510→    if tool_name == "vision_analyze":
   511→        return _wrap(f"┊ 👁️  vision    {_trunc(args.get('question', ''), 30)}  {dur}")
   512→    if tool_name == "mixture_of_agents":
   513→        return _wrap(f"┊ 🧠 reason    {_trunc(args.get('user_prompt', ''), 30)}  {dur}")
   514→    if tool_name == "send_message":
   515→        return _wrap(f"┊ 📨 send      {args.get('target', '?')}: \"{_trunc(args.get('message', ''), 25)}\"  {dur}")
   516→    if tool_name == "schedule_cronjob":
   517→        return _wrap(f"┊ ⏰ schedule  {_trunc(args.get('name', args.get('prompt', 'task')), 30)}  {dur}")
   518→    if tool_name == "list_cronjobs":
   519→        return _wrap(f"┊ ⏰ jobs      listing  {dur}")
   520→    if tool_name == "remove_cronjob":
   521→        return _wrap(f"┊ ⏰ remove    job {args.get('job_id', '?')}  {dur}")
   522→    if tool_name.startswith("rl_"):
   523→        rl = {
   524→            "rl_list_environments": "list envs", "rl_select_environment": f"select {args.get('name', '')}",
   525→            "rl_get_current_config": "get config", "rl_edit_config": f"set {args.get('field', '?')}",
   526→            "rl_start_training": "start training", "rl_check_status": f"status {args.get('run_id', '?')[:12]}",
   527→            "rl_stop_training": f"stop {args.get('run_id', '?')[:12]}", "rl_get_results": f"results {args.get('run_id', '?')[:12]}",
   528→            "rl_list_runs": "list runs", "rl_test_inference": "test inference",
   529→        }
   530→        return _wrap(f"┊ 🧪 rl        {rl.get(tool_name, tool_name.replace('rl_', ''))}  {dur}")
   531→    if tool_name == "execute_code":
   532→        code = args.get("code", "")
   533→        first_line = code.strip().split("\n")[0] if code.strip() else ""
   534→        return _wrap(f"┊ 🐍 exec      {_trunc(first_line, 35)}  {dur}")
   535→    if tool_name == "delegate_task":
   536→        tasks = args.get("tasks")
   537→        if tasks and isinstance(tasks, list):
   538→            return _wrap(f"┊ 🔀 delegate  {len(tasks)} parallel tasks  {dur}")
   539→        return _wrap(f"┊ 🔀 delegate  {_trunc(args.get('goal', ''), 35)}  {dur}")
   540→
   541→    preview = build_tool_preview(tool_name, args) or ""
   542→    return _wrap(f"┊ ⚡ {tool_name[:9]:9} {_trunc(preview, 35)}  {dur}")
   543→
   544→
   545→# =========================================================================
   546→# Honcho session line (one-liner with clickable OSC 8 hyperlink)
   547→# =========================================================================
   548→
   549→_DIM = "\033[2m"
   550→_SKY_BLUE = "\033[38;5;117m"
   551→_ANSI_RESET = "\033[0m"
   552→
   553→
   554→def honcho_session_url(workspace: str, session_name: str) -> str:
   555→    """Build a Honcho app URL for a session."""
   556→    from urllib.parse import quote
   557→    return (
   558→        f"https://app.honcho.dev/explore"
   559→        f"?workspace={quote(workspace, safe='')}"
   560→        f"&view=sessions"
   561→        f"&session={quote(session_name, safe='')}"
   562→    )
   563→
   564→
   565→def _osc8_link(url: str, text: str) -> str:
   566→    """OSC 8 terminal hyperlink (clickable in iTerm2, Ghostty, WezTerm, etc.)."""
   567→    return f"\033]8;;{url}\033\\{text}\033]8;;\033\\"
   568→
   569→
   570→def honcho_session_line(workspace: str, session_name: str) -> str:
   571→    """One-line session indicator: `Honcho session: <clickable name>`."""
   572→    url = honcho_session_url(workspace, session_name)
   573→    linked_name = _osc8_link(url, f"{_SKY_BLUE}{session_name}{_ANSI_RESET}")
   574→    return f"{_DIM}Honcho session:{_ANSI_RESET} {linked_name}"
   575→
   576→
   577→def write_tty(text: str) -> None:
   578→    """Write directly to /dev/tty, bypassing stdout capture."""
   579→    try:
   580→        fd = os.open("/dev/tty", os.O_WRONLY)
   581→        os.write(fd, text.encode("utf-8"))
   582→        os.close(fd)
   583→    except OSError:
   584→        sys.stdout.write(text)
   585→        sys.stdout.flush()
   586→

1→"""Shared curses-based UI components for Hermes CLI.
     2→
     3→Used by `hermes tools` and `hermes skills` for interactive checklists.
     4→Provides a curses multi-select with keyboard navigation, plus a
     5→text-based numbered fallback for terminals without curses support.
     6→"""
     7→from typing import List, Set
     8→
     9→from hermes_cli.colors import Colors, color
    10→
    11→
    12→def curses_checklist(
    13→    title: str,
    14→    items: List[str],
    15→    selected: Set[int],
    16→    *,
    17→    cancel_returns: Set[int] | None = None,
    18→) -> Set[int]:
    19→    """Curses multi-select checklist. Returns set of selected indices.
    20→
    21→    Args:
    22→        title: Header line displayed above the checklist.
    23→        items: Display labels for each row.
    24→        selected: Indices that start checked (pre-selected).
    25→        cancel_returns: Returned on ESC/q. Defaults to the original *selected*.
    26→    """
    27→    if cancel_returns is None:
    28→        cancel_returns = set(selected)
    29→
    30→    try:
    31→        import curses
    32→        chosen = set(selected)
    33→        result_holder: list = [None]
    34→
    35→        def _draw(stdscr):
    36→            curses.curs_set(0)
    37→            if curses.has_colors():
    38→                curses.start_color()
    39→                curses.use_default_colors()
    40→                curses.init_pair(1, curses.COLOR_GREEN, -1)
    41→                curses.init_pair(2, curses.COLOR_YELLOW, -1)
    42→                curses.init_pair(3, 8, -1)  # dim gray
    43→            cursor = 0
    44→            scroll_offset = 0
    45→
    46→            while True:
    47→                stdscr.clear()
    48→                max_y, max_x = stdscr.getmaxyx()
    49→
    50→                # Header
    51→                try:
    52→                    hattr = curses.A_BOLD
    53→                    if curses.has_colors():
    54→                        hattr |= curses.color_pair(2)
    55→                    stdscr.addnstr(0, 0, title, max_x - 1, hattr)
    56→                    stdscr.addnstr(
    57→                        1, 0,
    58→                        "  ↑↓ navigate  SPACE toggle  ENTER confirm  ESC cancel",
    59→                        max_x - 1, curses.A_DIM,
    60→                    )
    61→                except curses.error:
    62→                    pass
    63→
    64→                # Scrollable item list
    65→                visible_rows = max_y - 3
    66→                if cursor < scroll_offset:
    67→                    scroll_offset = cursor
    68→                elif cursor >= scroll_offset + visible_rows:
    69→                    scroll_offset = cursor - visible_rows + 1
    70→
    71→                for draw_i, i in enumerate(
    72→                    range(scroll_offset, min(len(items), scroll_offset + visible_rows))
    73→                ):
    74→                    y = draw_i + 3
    75→                    if y >= max_y - 1:
    76→                        break
    77→                    check = "✓" if i in chosen else " "
    78→                    arrow = "→" if i == cursor else " "
    79→                    line = f" {arrow} [{check}] {items[i]}"
    80→                    attr = curses.A_NORMAL
    81→                    if i == cursor:
    82→                        attr = curses.A_BOLD
    83→                        if curses.has_colors():
    84→                            attr |= curses.color_pair(1)
    85→                    try:
    86→                        stdscr.addnstr(y, 0, line, max_x - 1, attr)
    87→                    except curses.error:
    88→                        pass
    89→
    90→                stdscr.refresh()
    91→                key = stdscr.getch()
    92→
    93→                if key in (curses.KEY_UP, ord("k")):
    94→                    cursor = (cursor - 1) % len(items)
    95→                elif key in (curses.KEY_DOWN, ord("j")):
    96→                    cursor = (cursor + 1) % len(items)
    97→                elif key == ord(" "):
    98→                    chosen.symmetric_difference_update({cursor})
    99→                elif key in (curses.KEY_ENTER, 10, 13):
   100→                    result_holder[0] = set(chosen)
   101→                    return
   102→                elif key in (27, ord("q")):
   103→                    result_holder[0] = cancel_returns
   104→                    return
   105→
   106→        curses.wrapper(_draw)
   107→        return result_holder[0] if result_holder[0] is not None else cancel_returns
   108→
   109→    except Exception:
   110→        return _numbered_fallback(title, items, selected, cancel_returns)
   111→
   112→
   113→def _numbered_fallback(
   114→    title: str,
   115→    items: List[str],
   116→    selected: Set[int],
   117→    cancel_returns: Set[int],
   118→) -> Set[int]:
   119→    """Text-based toggle fallback for terminals without curses."""
   120→    chosen = set(selected)
   121→    print(color(f"\n  {title}", Colors.YELLOW))
   122→    print(color("  Toggle by number, Enter to confirm.\n", Colors.DIM))
   123→
   124→    while True:
   125→        for i, label in enumerate(items):
   126→            marker = color("[✓]", Colors.GREEN) if i in chosen else "[ ]"
   127→            print(f"  {marker} {i + 1:>2}. {label}")
   128→        print()
   129→        try:
   130→            val = input(color("  Toggle # (or Enter to confirm): ", Colors.DIM)).strip()
   131→            if not val:
   132→                break
   133→            idx = int(val) - 1
   134→            if 0 <= idx < len(items):
   135→                chosen.symmetric_difference_update({idx})
   136→        except (ValueError, KeyboardInterrupt, EOFError):
   137→            return cancel_returns
   138→        print()
   139→
   140→    return chosen
   141→
```

   688→# ============================================================================
   689→
   690→# Color palette (hex colors for Rich markup):
   691→# - Gold: #FFD700 (headers, highlights)
   692→# - Amber: #FFBF00 (secondary highlights)
   693→# - Bronze: #CD7F32 (tertiary elements)

   698→_GOLD = "\033[1;33m"    # Bold yellow — closest universal match to the gold theme
   699→_BOLD = "\033[1m"
   700→_DIM = "\033[2m"
   701→_RST = "\033[0m"
   702→
   703→def _cprint(text: str):
   704→    """Print ANSI-colored text through prompt_toolkit's native renderer.
   705→
   706→    Raw ANSI escapes written via print() are swallowed by patch_stdout's
   707→    StdoutProxy.  Routing through print_formatted_text(ANSI(...)) lets
   708→    prompt_toolkit parse the escapes and render real colors.
   709→    """
   710→    _pt_print(_PT_ANSI(text))
   711→
   712→
   713→class ChatConsole:
   714→    """Rich Console adapter for prompt_toolkit's patch_stdout context.
   715→
   716→    Captures Rich's rendered ANSI output and routes it through _cprint
   717→    so colors and markup render correctly inside the interactive chat loop.
   718→    Drop-in replacement for Rich Console — just pass this to any function
   719→    that expects a console.print() interface.
   720→    """
   721→
   722→    def __init__(self):
   723→        from io import StringIO
   724→        self._buffer = StringIO()
   725→        self._inner = Console(file=self._buffer, force_terminal=True, highlight=False)
   726→
   727→    def print(self, *args, **kwargs):
   728→        self._buffer.seek(0)
   729→        self._buffer.truncate()
   730→        # Read terminal width at render time so panels adapt to current size
   731→        self._inner.width = shutil.get_terminal_size((80, 24)).columns
   732→        self._inner.print(*args, **kwargs)
   733→        output = self._buffer.getvalue()
   734→        for line in output.rstrip("\n").split("\n"):
   735→            _cprint(line)
   736→
   737→def _skin_c(key: str, fallback: str = "") -> str:
   738→    """Get a skin color for Rich markup."""
   739→    try:
   740→        from hermes_cli.skin_engine import get_active_skin
   741→        return get_active_skin().get_color(key, fallback)
   742→    except Exception:
   743→        return fallback
   744→
   745→
   746→def _get_available_skills() -> Dict[str, List[str]]:
   747→    """
   748→    Scan ~/.hermes/skills/ and return skills grouped by category.
   749→    
   750→    Returns:
   751→        Dict mapping category name to list of skill names
   752→    """
   753→    import os
   754→    
   755→    hermes_home = Path(os.getenv("HERMES_HOME", Path.home() / ".hermes"))
   756→    skills_dir = hermes_home / "skills"
   757→    skills_by_category = {}
   758→    
   759→    if not skills_dir.exists():
   760→        return skills_by_category
   761→    
   762→    for skill_file in skills_dir.rglob("SKILL.md"):
   763→        rel_path = skill_file.relative_to(skills_dir)
   764→        parts = rel_path.parts
   765→        
   766→        if len(parts) >= 2:
   767→            category = parts[0]
   768→            skill_name = parts[-2]
   769→        else:
   770→            category = "general"
   771→            skill_name = skill_file.parent.name
   772→        
   773→        skills_by_category.setdefault(category, []).append(skill_name)
   774→    
   775→    return skills_by_category
   776→
   777→
   778→def _format_context_length(tokens: int) -> str:
   779→    """Format a token count for display (e.g. 128000 → '128K', 1048576 → '1M')."""
   780→    if tokens >= 1_000_000:
   781→        val = tokens / 1_000_000
   782→        return f"{val:g}M"
   783→    elif tokens >= 1_000:
   784→        val = tokens / 1_000
   785→        return f"{val:g}K"
   786→    return str(tokens)
   787→
   788→
   789→def build_welcome_banner(console: Console, model: str, cwd: str, tools: List[dict] = None, enabled_toolsets: List[str] = None, session_id: str = None, context_length: int = None):
   790→    """
   791→    Build and print a Claude Code-style welcome banner with caduceus on left and info on right.
   792→    
   793→    Args:
   794→        console: Rich Console instance for printing
   795→        model: The current model name (e.g., "anthropic/claude-opus-4")
   796→        cwd: Current working directory
   797→        tools: List of tool definitions
   798→        enabled_toolsets: List of enabled toolset names
   799→        session_id: Unique session identifier for logging
   800→        context_length: Model's context window size in tokens
   801→    """
   802→    from model_tools import check_tool_availability
   803→    
   804→    tools = tools or []
   805→    enabled_toolsets = enabled_toolsets or []
   806→    
   807→    # Get unavailable tools info for coloring
   808→    _, unavailable_toolsets = check_tool_availability(quiet=True)
   809→    disabled_tools = set()
   810→    for item in unavailable_toolsets:
   811→        disabled_tools.update(item.get("tools", []))
   812→    
   813→    # Build the side-by-side content using a table for precise control
   814→    layout_table = Table.grid(padding=(0, 2))
   815→    layout_table.add_column("left", justify="center")
   816→    layout_table.add_column("right", justify="left")
   817→    
   818→    # Build left content: caduceus + model info
   819→    # Resolve skin colors for the banner
   820→    try:
   821→        from hermes_cli.skin_engine import get_active_skin, get_theme_mode
   822→        _bskin = get_active_skin()
   823→        _theme = get_theme_mode()
   824→        _accent = _bskin.get_color("banner_accent", "#FFBF00")
   825→        _dim = _bskin.get_color("banner_dim", "#B8860B")
   826→        _text = _bskin.get_color("banner_text", "#FFF8DC")
   827→        _session_c = _bskin.get_color("session_border", "#8B8682")
   828→        _title_c = _bskin.get_color("banner_title", "#FFD700")
   829→        _border_c = _bskin.get_color("banner_border", "#CD7F32")
   830→        _agent_name = _bskin.get_branding("agent_name", "Hermes Agent")
   831→    except Exception:
   832→        _bskin = None
   833→        _accent, _dim, _text = "#FFBF00", "#B8860B", "#FFF8DC"
   834→        _session_c, _title_c, _border_c = "#8B8682", "#FFD700", "#CD7F32"
   835→        _agent_name = "Hermes Agent"
   836→

   987→# ============================================================================
   988→# HermesCLI Class

   990→
   991→class HermesCLI:
   992→    """
   993→    Interactive CLI for the Hermes Agent.
   994→    
   995→    Provides a REPL interface with rich formatting, command history,
   996→    and tool execution capabilities.
   997→    """
   998→    
   999→    def __init__(
  1000→        self,
  1001→        model: str = None,
  1002→        toolsets: List[str] = None,
  1003→        provider: str = None,
  1004→        api_key: str = None,
  1005→        base_url: str = None,
  1006→        max_turns: int = None,
  1007→        verbose: bool = False,
  1008→        compact: bool = False,
  1009→        resume: str = None,
  1010→        checkpoints: bool = False,
  1011→        pass_session_id: bool = False,
  1012→    ):
  1013→        """
  1014→        Initialize the Hermes CLI.
  1015→
  1016→        Args:
  1017→            model: Model to use (default: from env or claude-sonnet)
  1018→            toolsets: List of toolsets to enable (default: all)
  1019→            provider: Inference provider ("auto", "openrouter", "nous", "openai-codex", "zai", "kimi-coding", "minimax", "minimax-cn")
  1020→            api_key: API key (default: from environment)
  1021→            base_url: API base URL (default: OpenRouter)
  1022→            max_turns: Maximum tool-calling iterations shared with subagents (default: 90)
  1023→            verbose: Enable verbose logging
  1024→            compact: Use compact display mode
  1025→            resume: Session ID to resume (restores conversation history from SQLite)
  1026→            pass_session_id: Include the session ID in the agent's system prompt
  1027→        """
  1028→        # Initialize Rich console
  1029→        self.console = Console()
  1030→        self.config = CLI_CONFIG
  1031→        self.compact = compact if compact is not None else CLI_CONFIG["display"].get("compact", False)
  1032→        # tool_progress: "off", "new", "all", "verbose" (from config.yaml display section)
  1033→        self.tool_progress_mode = CLI_CONFIG["display"].get("tool_progress", "all")
  1034→        # resume_display: "full" (show history) | "minimal" (one-liner only)
  1035→        self.resume_display = CLI_CONFIG["display"].get("resume_display", "full")
  1036→        # bell_on_complete: play terminal bell (\a) when agent finishes a response
  1037→        self.bell_on_complete = CLI_CONFIG["display"].get("bell_on_complete", False)
  1038→        # show_reasoning: display model thinking/reasoning before the response
  1039→        self.show_reasoning = CLI_CONFIG["display"].get("show_reasoning", False)
  1040→        self.verbose = verbose if verbose is not None else (self.tool_progress_mode == "verbose")
  1041→        
  1042→        # Configuration - priority: CLI args > env vars > config file
  1043→        # Model comes from: CLI arg or config.yaml (single source of truth).
  1044→        # LLM_MODEL/OPENAI_MODEL env vars are NOT checked — config.yaml is
  1045→        # authoritative.  This avoids conflicts in multi-agent setups where
  1046→        # env vars would stomp each other.
  1047→        _model_config = CLI_CONFIG.get("model", {})
  1048→        _config_model = _model_config.get("default", "") if isinstance(_model_config, dict) else (_model_config or "")
  1049→        self.model = model or _config_model or "anthropic/claude-opus-4.6"
  1050→        # Track whether model was explicitly chosen by the user or fell back
  1051→        # to the global default.  Provider-specific normalisation may override
  1052→        # the default silently but should warn when overriding an explicit choice.
  1053→        self._model_is_default = not model
  1054→
  1055→        self._explicit_api_key = api_key
  1056→        self._explicit_base_url = base_url
  1057→
  1058→        # Provider selection is resolved lazily at use-time via _ensure_runtime_credentials().
  1059→        self.requested_provider = (
  1060→            provider
  1061→            or os.getenv("HERMES_INFERENCE_PROVIDER")
  1062→            or CLI_CONFIG["model"].get("provider")
  1063→            or "auto"
  1064→        )
  1065→        self._provider_source: Optional[str] = None
  1066→        self.provider = self.requested_provider
  1067→        self.api_mode = "chat_completions"
  1068→        self.base_url = (
  1069→            base_url
  1070→            or os.getenv("OPENAI_BASE_URL")

  1082→        # Max turns priority: CLI arg > config file > env var > default
  1083→        if max_turns is not None:  # CLI arg was explicitly set
  1084→            self.max_turns = max_turns

  4122→        from prompt_toolkit.keys import Keys
  4123→
  4124→        @kb.add(Keys.BracketedPaste, eager=True)
  4125→        def handle_paste(event):
  4126→            """Handle terminal paste — detect clipboard images.
  4127→
  4128→            When the terminal supports bracketed paste, Ctrl+V / Cmd+V
  4129→            triggers this with the pasted text.  We also check the
  4130→            clipboard for an image on every paste event.
  4131→            """
  4132→            pasted_text = event.data or ""
  4133→            if self._try_attach_clipboard_image():
  4134→                event.app.invalidate()
  4135→            if pasted_text:
  4136→                event.current_buffer.insert_text(pasted_text)
  4137→
  4138→        @kb.add('c-v')
  4139→        def handle_ctrl_v(event):
  4140→            """Fallback image paste for terminals without bracketed paste.
  4141→
  4142→            On Linux terminals (GNOME Terminal, Konsole, etc.), Ctrl+V
  4143→            sends raw byte 0x16 instead of triggering a paste.  This
  4144→            binding catches that and checks the clipboard for images.
  4145→            On terminals that DO intercept Ctrl+V for paste (macOS
  4146→            Terminal, iTerm2, VSCode, Windows Terminal), the bracketed
  4147→            paste handler fires instead and this binding never triggers.
  4148→            """
  4149→            if self._try_attach_clipboard_image():
  4150→                event.app.invalidate()
  4151→
  4152→        @kb.add('escape', 'v')
  4153→        def handle_alt_v(event):
  4154→            """Alt+V — paste image from clipboard.
  4155→
  4156→            Alt key combos pass through all terminal emulators (sent as
  4157→            ESC + key), unlike Ctrl+V which terminals intercept for text
  4158→            paste.  This is the reliable way to attach clipboard images
  4159→            on WSL2, VSCode, and any terminal over SSH where Ctrl+V
  4160→            can't reach the application for image-only clipboard.
  4161→            """
  4162→            if self._try_attach_clipboard_image():
  4163→                event.app.invalidate()
  4164→            else:
  4165→                # No image found — show a hint
  4166→                pass  # silent when no image (avoid noise on accidental press)

  4175→            if cli_ref._secret_state:

  4185→            if cli_ref._agent_running:

  4202→        # Dynamic height: accounts for both explicit newlines AND visual
  4203→        # wrapping of long lines so the input area always fits its content.
  4204→        # The prompt characters ("❯ " etc.) consume ~4 columns.
  4205→        def _input_height():
  4206→            try:
  4207→                doc = input_area.buffer.document
  4208→                available_width = shutil.get_terminal_size().columns - 4  # subtract prompt width
  4209→                if available_width < 10:
  4210→                    available_width = 40
  4211→                visual_lines = 0
  4212→                for line in doc.lines:
  4213→                    # Each logical line takes at least 1 visual row; long lines wrap
  4214→                    if len(line) == 0:
  4215→                        visual_lines += 1
  4216→                    else:
  4217→                        visual_lines += max(1, -(-len(line) // available_width))  # ceil division
  4218→                return min(max(visual_lines, 1), 8)
  4219→            except Exception:
  4220→                return 1
  4221→
  4222→        input_area.window.height = _input_height
  4223→
  4224→        # Paste collapsing: detect large pastes and save to temp file
  4225→        _paste_counter = [0]
  4226→        _prev_text_len = [0]
  4227→
  4228→        def _on_text_changed(buf):
  4229→            """Detect large pastes and collapse them to a file reference."""
  4230→            text = buf.text
  4231→            line_count = text.count('\n')
  4232→            chars_added = len(text) - _prev_text_len[0]
  4233→            _prev_text_len[0] = len(text)
  4234→            # Heuristic: a real paste adds many characters at once (not just a
  4235→            # single newline from Alt+Enter) AND the result has 5+ lines.
  4236→            if line_count >= 5 and chars_added > 1 and not text.startswith('/'):
  4237→                _paste_counter[0] += 1
  4238→                # Save to temp file
  4239→                paste_dir = Path(os.path.expanduser("~/.hermes/pastes"))
  4240→                paste_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  4241→                paste_file = paste_dir / f"paste_{_paste_counter[0]}_{datetime.now().strftime('%H%M%S')}.txt"
  4242→                paste_file.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
  4243→                # Replace buffer with compact reference
  4244→                buf.text = f"[Pasted text #{_paste_counter[0]}: {line_count + 1} lines → {paste_file}]"
  4245→                buf.cursor_position = len(buf.text)
  4246→
  4247→        input_area.buffer.on_text_changed += _on_text_changed
  4248→

  4260→
  4261→        class _PlaceholderProcessor(Processor):
  4262→            """Render grayed-out placeholder text inside the input when empty."""
  4263→            def __init__(self, get_text):
  4264→                self._get_text = get_text
  4265→
  4266→            def apply_transformation(self, ti):
  4267→                if not ti.document.text and ti.lineno == 0:
  4268→                    text = self._get_text()
  4269→                    if text:
  4270→                        # Append after existing fragments (preserves the ❯ prompt)
  4271→                        return Transformation(fragments=ti.fragments + [('class:placeholder', text)])
  4272→                return Transformation(fragments=ti.fragments)
  4273→
  4274→        def _get_placeholder():
  4275→            if cli_ref._sudo_state:
  4276→                return "type password (hidden), Enter to skip"
  4277→            if cli_ref._secret_state:
  4278→                return "type secret (hidden), Enter to skip"
  4279→            if cli_ref._approval_state:
  4280→                return ""
  4281→            if cli_ref._clarify_freetext:
  4282→                return "type your answer here and press Enter"
  4283→            if cli_ref._clarify_state:
  4284→                return ""
  4285→            if cli_ref._command_running:
  4286→                frame = cli_ref._command_spinner_frame()
  4287→                status = cli_ref._command_status or "Processing command..."
  4288→                return f"{frame} {status}"
  4289→            if cli_ref._agent_running:
  4290→                return "type a message + Enter to interrupt, Ctrl+C to cancel"
  4291→            return ""
  4292→
  4293→        input_area.control.input_processors.append(_PlaceholderProcessor(_get_placeholder))
  4294→
  4295→        # Hint line above input: shown only for interactive prompts that need

  4297→        # The agent-running interrupt hint is now an inline placeholder above.
  4298→        def get_hint_text():
  4299→            import time as _time

  4301→            if cli_ref._sudo_state:

  4303→                return [
  4304→                    ('class:hint', '  password hidden · Enter to skip'),
  4305→                    ('class:clarify-countdown', f'  ({remaining}s)'),
  4306→                ]
  4307→
  4308→            if cli_ref._secret_state:
  4309→                remaining = max(0, int(cli_ref._secret_deadline - _time.monotonic()))
  4310→                return [
  4311→                    ('class:hint', '  secret hidden · Enter to skip'),

  4313→                ]
  4314→
  4315→            if cli_ref._approval_state:
  4316→                remaining = max(0, int(cli_ref._approval_deadline - _time.monotonic()))
  4317→                return [
  4318→                    ('class:hint', '  ↑/↓ to select, Enter to confirm'),

  4321→
  4322→            if cli_ref._clarify_state:
  4323→                remaining = max(0, int(cli_ref._clarify_deadline - _time.monotonic()))
  4324→                countdown = f'  ({remaining}s)' if cli_ref._clarify_deadline else ''
  4325→                if cli_ref._clarify_freetext:
  4326→                    return [

  4330→                return [

  4334→
  4335→            if cli_ref._command_running:

  4341→            return []

  4371→        def _panel_box_width(title: str, content_lines: list[str], min_width: int = 46, max_width: int = 76) -> int:
  4372→            """Choose a stable panel width wide enough for the title and content."""
  4373→            term_cols = shutil.get_terminal_size((100, 20)).columns
  4374→            longest = max([len(title)] + [len(line) for line in content_lines] + [min_width - 4])
  4375→            inner = min(max(longest + 4, min_width - 2), max_width - 2, max(24, term_cols - 6))
  4376→            return inner + 2  # account for the single leading/trailing spaces inside borders
  4377→
  4378→        def _wrap_panel_text(text: str, width: int, subsequent_indent: str = "") -> list[str]:
  4379→            wrapped = textwrap.wrap(
  4380→                text,
  4381→                width=max(8, width),
  4382→                break_long_words=False,
  4383→                break_on_hyphens=False,
  4384→                subsequent_indent=subsequent_indent,
  4385→            )
  4386→            return wrapped or [""]

  4388→        def _append_panel_line(lines, border_style: str, content_style: str, text: str, box_width: int) -> None:
  4389→            inner_width = max(0, box_width - 2)
  4390→            lines.append((border_style, "│ "))
  4391→            lines.append((content_style, text.ljust(inner_width)))
  4392→            lines.append((border_style, " │\n"))
  4393→
  4394→        def _append_blank_panel_line(lines, border_style: str, box_width: int) -> None:
  4395→            lines.append((border_style, "│" + (" " * box_width) + "│\n"))

1→"""Hermes CLI skin/theme engine.
     2→
     3→A data-driven skin system that lets users customize the CLI's visual appearance.
     4→Skins are defined as YAML files in ~/.hermes/skins/ or as built-in presets.
     5→No code changes are needed to add a new skin.
     6→
     7→SKIN YAML SCHEMA
     8→================
     9→
    10→All fields are optional. Missing values inherit from the ``default`` skin.
    11→
    12→.. code-block:: yaml
    13→
    14→    # Required: skin identity
    15→    name: mytheme                         # Unique skin name (lowercase, hyphens ok)
    16→    description: Short description        # Shown in /skin listing
    17→
    18→    # Colors: hex values for Rich markup (banner, UI, response box)
    19→    # These are the default (dark terminal) colors.
    20→    colors:
    21→      banner_border: "#CD7F32"            # Panel border color
    22→      banner_title: "#FFD700"             # Panel title text color
    23→      banner_accent: "#FFBF00"            # Section headers (Available Tools, etc.)
    24→      banner_dim: "#B8860B"               # Dim/muted text (separators, labels)
    25→      banner_text: "#FFF8DC"              # Body text (tool names, skill names)
    26→      ui_accent: "#FFBF00"               # General UI accent
    27→      ui_label: "#4dd0e1"                # UI labels
    28→      ui_ok: "#4caf50"                   # Success indicators
    29→      ui_error: "#ef5350"                # Error indicators
    30→      ui_warn: "#ffa726"                 # Warning indicators
    31→      prompt: "#FFF8DC"                  # Prompt text color
    32→      input_rule: "#CD7F32"              # Input area horizontal rule
    33→      response_border: "#FFD700"         # Response box border (ANSI)
    34→      session_label: "#DAA520"           # Session label color
    35→      session_border: "#8B8682"          # Session ID dim color
    36→
    37→    # Light-mode color overrides (optional). Only keys that need to change.
    38→    # When theme_mode is "light", these replace the corresponding colors above.
    39→    colors_light:
    40→      banner_title: "#B8860B"
    41→      prompt: "#5C4A00"
    42→
    43→    # Spinner: customize the animated spinner during API calls
    44→    spinner:
    45→      waiting_faces:                      # Faces shown while waiting for API
    46→        - "(⚔)"
    47→        - "(⛨)"
    48→      thinking_faces:                     # Faces shown during reasoning
    49→        - "(⌁)"
    50→        - "(<>)"
    51→      thinking_verbs:                     # Verbs for spinner messages
    52→        - "forging"
    53→        - "plotting"
    54→      wings:                              # Optional left/right spinner decorations
    55→        - ["⟪⚔", "⚔⟫"]                  # Each entry is [left, right] pair
    56→        - ["⟪▲", "▲⟫"]
    57→
    58→    # Branding: text strings used throughout the CLI
    59→    branding:
    60→      agent_name: "Hermes Agent"          # Banner title, status display
    61→      welcome: "Welcome message"          # Shown at CLI startup
    62→      goodbye: "Goodbye! ⚕"              # Shown on exit
    63→      response_label: " ⚕ Hermes "       # Response box header label
    64→      prompt_symbol: "❯ "                # Input prompt symbol
    65→      help_header: "(^_^)? Commands"      # /help header text
    66→
    67→    # Tool prefix: character for tool output lines (default: ┊)
    68→    tool_prefix: "┊"
    69→
    70→USAGE
    71→=====
    72→
    73→.. code-block:: python
    74→
    75→    from hermes_cli.skin_engine import get_active_skin, list_skins, set_active_skin
    76→
    77→    skin = get_active_skin()
    78→    print(skin.colors["banner_title"])    # "#FFD700"
    79→    print(skin.get_branding("agent_name"))  # "Hermes Agent"
    80→
    81→    set_active_skin("ares")               # Switch to built-in ares skin
    82→    set_active_skin("mytheme")            # Switch to user skin from ~/.hermes/skins/
    83→
    84→BUILT-IN SKINS
    85→==============
    86→
    87→- ``default`` — Classic Hermes gold/kawaii (the current look)
    88→- ``ares``    — Crimson/bronze war-god theme with custom spinner wings
    89→- ``mono``    — Clean grayscale monochrome
    90→- ``slate``   — Cool blue developer-focused theme
    91→
    92→USER SKINS
    93→==========
    94→
    95→Drop a YAML file in ``~/.hermes/skins/<name>.yaml`` following the schema above.
    96→Activate with ``/skin <name>`` in the CLI or ``display.skin: <name>`` in config.yaml.
    97→"""
    98→
    99→import logging
   100→import os

1→"""Welcome banner, ASCII art, skills summary, and update check for the CLI.
     2→
     3→Pure display functions with no HermesCLI state dependency.
     4→"""
     5→
     6→import json
     7→import logging
     8→import os
     9→import subprocess
    10→import time
    11→from pathlib import Path
    12→from typing import Dict, List, Optional
    13→
    14→from rich.console import Console
    15→from rich.panel import Panel
    16→from rich.table import Table
    17→
    18→from prompt_toolkit import print_formatted_text as _pt_print
    19→from prompt_toolkit.formatted_text import ANSI as _PT_ANSI
    20→
    21→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    22→
    23→
    24→# =========================================================================
    25→# ANSI building blocks for conversation display
    26→# =========================================================================
    27→
    28→_GOLD = "\033[1;33m"
    29→_BOLD = "\033[1m"
    30→_DIM = "\033[2m"
    31→_RST = "\033[0m"
    32→
    33→
    34→def cprint(text: str):
    35→    """Print ANSI-colored text through prompt_toolkit's renderer."""
    36→    _pt_print(_PT_ANSI(text))
    37→
    38→
    39→# =========================================================================
    40→# Skin-aware color helpers
    41→# =========================================================================
    42→
    43→def _skin_color(key: str, fallback: str) -> str:
    44→    """Get a color from the active skin, or return fallback."""
    45→    try:
    46→        from hermes_cli.skin_engine import get_active_skin
    47→        return get_active_skin().get_color(key, fallback)
    48→    except Exception:
    49→        return fallback
    50→
    51→
    52→def _skin_branding(key: str, fallback: str) -> str:
    53→    """Get a branding string from the active skin, or return fallback."""
    54→    try:
    55→        from hermes_cli.skin_engine import get_active_skin
    56→        return get_active_skin().get_branding(key, fallback)
    57→    except Exception:
    58→        return fallback
    59→
    60→
    61→# =========================================================================
    62→# ASCII Art & Branding

   103→def _build_compact_banner() -> str:
   104→    """Build the compact banner using active skin colors."""
   105→    title = _skin_color("banner_title", "#FFD700")
   106→    accent = _skin_color("banner_accent", "#FFBF00")
   107→    border = _skin_color("banner_border", "#CD7F32")
   108→    dim = _skin_color("banner_dim", "#B8860B")
   109→    return f"""
   110→[bold {title}]╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗[/]
   111→[bold {title}]║[/]  [{accent}]⚕ NOUS HERMES[/] [dim {dim}]- AI Agent Framework[/]              [bold {title}]║[/]
   112→[bold {title}]║[/]  [{border}]Messenger of the Digital Gods[/]    [dim {dim}]Nous Research[/]   [bold {title}]║[/]
   113→[bold {title}]╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝[/]
   114→"""
   115→
   116→
   117→# Backward-compatible aliases (now dynamic)
   118→HERMES_AGENT_LOGO = None  # Use _build_hermes_logo() instead
   119→HERMES_CADUCEUS = None    # Use _build_hermes_caduceus() instead
   120→COMPACT_BANNER = None     # Use _build_compact_banner() instead
   121→
   122→
   123→# =========================================================================
   124→# Skills scanning
   125→# =========================================================================
   126→
   127→def get_available_skills() -> Dict[str, List[str]]:
   128→    """Scan ~/.hermes/skills/ and return skills grouped by category."""
   129→    import os
   130→
   131→    hermes_home = Path(os.getenv("HERMES_HOME", Path.home() / ".hermes"))
   132→    skills_dir = hermes_home / "skills"
   133→    skills_by_category = {}
   134→
   135→    if not skills_dir.exists():
   136→        return skills_by_category
   137→
   138→    for skill_file in skills_dir.rglob("SKILL.md"):
   139→        rel_path = skill_file.relative_to(skills_dir)
   140→        parts = rel_path.parts
   141→        if len(parts) >= 2:
   142→            category = parts[0]
   143→            skill_name = parts[-2]
   144→        else:
   145→            category = "general"
   146→            skill_name = skill_file.parent.name
   147→        skills_by_category.setdefault(category, []).append(skill_name)
   148→
   149→    return skills_by_category
   150→
```

  4122→        from prompt_toolkit.keys import Keys
  4123→
  4124→        @kb.add(Keys.BracketedPaste, eager=True)
  4125→        def handle_paste(event):
  4126→            """Handle terminal paste — detect clipboard images.
  4127→
  4128→            When the terminal supports bracketed paste, Ctrl+V / Cmd+V
  4129→            triggers this with the pasted text.  We also check the
  4130→            clipboard for an image on every paste event.
  4131→            """
  4132→            pasted_text = event.data or ""
  4133→            if self._try_attach_clipboard_image():
  4134→                event.app.invalidate()
  4135→            if pasted_text:
  4136→                event.current_buffer.insert_text(pasted_text)
  4137→
  4138→        @kb.add('c-v')
  4139→        def handle_ctrl_v(event):
  4140→            """Fallback image paste for terminals without bracketed paste.
  4141→
  4142→            On Linux terminals (GNOME Terminal, Konsole, etc.), Ctrl+V
  4143→            sends raw byte 0x16 instead of triggering a paste.  This
  4144→            binding catches that and checks the clipboard for images.
  4145→            On terminals that DO intercept Ctrl+V for paste (macOS
  4146→            Terminal, iTerm2, VSCode, Windows Terminal), the bracketed
  4147→            paste handler fires instead and this binding never triggers.
  4148→            """
  4149→            if self._try_attach_clipboard_image():
  4150→                event.app.invalidate()
  4151→
  4152→        @kb.add('escape', 'v')
  4153→        def handle_alt_v(event):
  4154→            """Alt+V — paste image from clipboard.
  4155→
  4156→            Alt key combos pass through all terminal emulators (sent as
  4157→            ESC + key), unlike Ctrl+V which terminals intercept for text
  4158→            paste.  This is the reliable way to attach clipboard images
  4159→            on WSL2, VSCode, and any terminal over SSH where Ctrl+V
  4160→            can't reach the application for image-only clipboard.
  4161→            """
  4162→            if self._try_attach_clipboard_image():
  4163→                event.app.invalidate()
  4164→            else:
  4165→                # No image found — show a hint
  4166→                pass  # silent when no image (avoid noise on accidental press)
  4167→

  4170→        cli_ref = self
  4171→
  4172→        def get_prompt():
  4173→            if cli_ref._sudo_state:
  4174→                return [('class:sudo-prompt', '🔐 ❯ ')]
  4175→            if cli_ref._secret_state:
  4176→                return [('class:sudo-prompt', '🔑 ❯ ')]
  4177→            if cli_ref._approval_state:
  4178→                return [('class:prompt-working', '⚠ ❯ ')]
  4179→            if cli_ref._clarify_freetext:
  4180→                return [('class:clarify-selected', '✎ ❯ ')]
  4181→            if cli_ref._clarify_state:
  4182→                return [('class:prompt-working', '? ❯ ')]
  4183→            if cli_ref._command_running:
  4184→                return [('class:prompt-working', f"{cli_ref._command_spinner_frame()} ❯ ")]
  4185→            if cli_ref._agent_running:
  4186→                return [('class:prompt-working', '⚕ ❯ ')]
  4187→            return [('class:prompt', '❯ ')]
  4188→
  4189→        # Create the input area with multiline (shift+enter), autocomplete, and paste handling
  4190→        input_area = TextArea(

## Hermes Agent Terminal UI Overview

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here's what the Hermes agent terminal UI looks like:

### 1. CLI Entry Point & Launcher

**Main Entry Point**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/cli.py`
- Also exposed via `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/hermes_cli/main.py` (the package's CLI entry point)
- Launched with: `python cli.py` or `hermes` command (via hermes_cli package)

**Key Launch Options**:
```
hermes                          # Start interactive mode
hermes -q "your question"       # Single query mode
hermes --toolsets web,terminal  # Specific toolsets
hermes --list-tools             # List available tools
hermes --resume <session_id>    # Resume previous session
```

### 2. Terminal Rendering & Formatting

**Framework**: Uses **prompt_toolkit** with Rich Console integration
- `prompt_toolkit.application.Application` for the interactive TUI
- `prompt_toolkit.layout.Layout` with HSplit for multi-panel layout
- `rich.console.Console` for rich text formatting
- Custom `patch_stdout()` context for clean output handling during agent processing

**Color Palette** (Gold/Bronze/Kawaii theme):
- Gold: `#FFD700` (headers, highlights)
- Amber: `#FFBF00` (secondary highlights)
- Bronze: `#CD7F32` (borders, tertiary elements)
- Light: `#FFF8DC` (body text)
- Dim: `#B8860B` (muted text)

These are managed via a skin engine (`hermes_cli/skin_engine.py`) that supports multiple themes (default, ares, mono, slate) and light/dark mode.

### 3. Welcome Banner

**Visual Design**: Claude Code-style two-column layout with ASCII art
- **Left Column**: Hermes caduceus symbol (mercury staff) + model info
- **Right Column**: Available tools grouped by toolset + skills list

**Caduceus ASCII Art** (15 lines, Braille character art):
```
⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⢀⣀⡀⠀⣀⣀⠀⢀⣀⡀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀
⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⢀⣠⣴⣾⣿⣿⣇⠸⣿⣿⠇⣸⣿⣿⣷⣦⣄⡀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀
⠀⢀⣠⣴⣶⠿⠋⣩⡿⣿⡿⠻⣿⡇⢠⡄⢸⣿⠟⢿⣿⢿⣍⠙⠿⣶⣦⣄⡀⠀
...
(Mercury staff with wings, symbolic of the messenger god Hermes)
```

**Large ASCII Logo** (for wider terminals):
```
██╗  ██╗███████╗██████╗ ███╗   ███╗███████╗███████╗       █████╗  ██████╗ ███████╗███╗   ██╗████████╗
██║  ██║██╔════╝██╔══██╗████╗ ████║██╔════╝██╔════╝      ██╔══██╗██╔════╝ ██╔════╝████╗  ██║╚══██╔══╝
███████║█████╗  ██████╔╝██╔████╔██║█████╗  ███████╗█████╗███████║██║  ███╗█████╗  ██╔██╗ ██║   ██║
...
```

**Display Features**:
- Wrapped in a Rich Panel with bordered frame
- Model name, context window, provider info displayed
- Session ID shown (clickable OSC 8 hyperlink for supported terminals)
- Tool availability status (disabled tools shown in red)
- Skills organized by category with counts
- Help command hint: `/help for commands`

**Banner Files**:
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/hermes_cli/banner.py` - Contains build functions
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/cli.py` - Main banner rendering (lines 789-971)

### 4. Interactive Input Prompt

**Layout Structure**:
```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────┐
│         [Welcome Banner & Output]        │
│                                           │
├─ Hint Line (instructions/countdown) ────┤
├─ Spinner Text (async operation status) ─┤
├─────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ ❯ [Input Area - multiline, 1-8 rows]   │
│   (autocomplete, placeholder text)      │
└─────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

**Dynamic Prompt Symbols** (changes based on state):
- `❯ ` - Normal idle state (class:prompt)
- `⚕ ❯ ` - Agent running/thinking (class:prompt-working)
- `? ❯ ` - Clarify question waiting for response (class:prompt-working)
- `✎ ❯ ` - Clarify freetext mode (class:clarify-selected)
- `⚠ ❯ ` - Approval confirmation (class:prompt-working)
- `🔐 ❯ ` - Sudo password prompt (class:sudo-prompt)
- `🔑 ❯ ` - Secret/credential capture (class:sudo-prompt)
- Animated spinner when processing (e.g., `⠋ ❯ `)

**Input Features**:
- Multiline support (Shift+Enter for new line, Alt+Enter alternative)
- Command history with FileHistory persistence
- Autocomplete for slash commands (dynamic, includes installed skills)
- Placeholder text that fades (e.g., "type a message to chat with Hermes")
- Image paste support (Alt+V, Ctrl+V, or `/paste` command)
- Password masking for sudo/secrets
- Dynamic height (1-8 rows based on content wrapping)

**Input Area Styles** (from `PTStyle.from_dict` around line 4687):
```python
'input-area': prompt_color
'placeholder': dim_color + ' italic'
'prompt': prompt_color
'prompt-working': '#888888 italic'
'hint': dim_color + ' italic'
'input-rule': input_rule_color
'image-badge': ui_label_color + ' bold'
```

### 5. User Input Display

When user submits a message, it's shown in the output as:
```
● Your message text here
  📎 2 images attached (if applicable)
```

**Formatting Details**:
- Gold bullet (●) as prefix
- Bold text for message content
- Dim text for line counts on multiline input
- Image attachment count shown with paperclip emoji
- Multi-line messages show: `(+N lines)` notation

**Implementation** (lines 4790-4803):
```python
_cprint(f"{_GOLD}●{_RST} {_BOLD}{user_input}{_RST}")
_cprint(f"  {_DIM}📎 {n} image{'s' if n > 1 else ''} attached{_RST}")
```

### 6. Agent Response Display

**Response Box Format**:
- Wrapped in a Rich `Panel` with horizontal lines (rich_box.HORIZONTALS)
- Title: `⚕ Hermes` (or custom from skin)
- Border color: Bronze (#CD7F32) by default, customizable via skin
- Padding: 1 line top/bottom, 2 spaces left/right
- Title alignment: left

**Sample Response**:
```
╭─ ⚕ Hermes ───────────────────────────────────────╮
│                                                    │
│ Here's the response text from the agent, which    │
│ can include multiple paragraphs and formatting.   │
│                                                    │
╰────────────────────────────────────────────────────╯
```

### 7. Tool Execution Display

**Spinner**: Animated during tool execution (Braille character animation)
```
Frames: ⠋ ⠙ ⠹ ⠸ ⠼ ⠴ ⠦ ⠧ ⠇ ⠏
Duration: 0.12s per frame
```

**Tool Progress Lines** (one per tool call):
```
┊ 🔍 search    "user query text"  1.2s
┊ 💻 $         npm install  0.8s
┊ 📖 read      src/main.py  0.3s
┊ ✍️  write     output.txt  0.2s
```

**Format**: `| {emoji} {verb:9} {detail}  {duration}`
- Prefix character: `┊` (customizable via skin)
- Tool-specific emoji: 🔍, 💻, 📖, ✍️, 🔧, 🌐, 👁️, etc.
- Verb: left-padded to 9 chars (search, $, read, write, patch, navigate, vision...)
- Detail: Tool-specific preview (truncated to ~40 chars)
- Duration: Elapsed time in seconds
- Failure suffix: `[exit N]`, `[error]`, or `[full]` (red color)

**Available Tool Icons** (from `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/agent/display.py`):
- 🔍 search (web_search)
- 📄 fetch (web_extract)
- 🕸️ crawl (web_crawl)
- 💻 $ (terminal)
- ⚙️ proc (process)
- 📖 read (read_file)
- ✍️ write (write_file)
- 🔧 patch (patch)
- 🔎 grep (search_files)
- 🌐 navigate (browser_navigate)
- 📸 snapshot (browser_snapshot)
- 👆 click (browser_click)
- ⌨️ type (browser_type)
- 🎨 create (image_generate)
- 🔊 speak (text_to_speech)
- 👁️ vision (vision_analyze)
- 📋 plan (todo/tasks)
- 🧠 reason (mixture_of_agents, memory)
- ⏰ schedule (schedule_cronjob)
- 🐍 exec (execute_code)

### 14. Notable UI Features

- **Responsive Layout**: Terminal width detection, dynamic text wrapping, responsive spinner
- **Thread-Safe Display**: Uses `patch_stdout()` for clean rendering during background operations
- **Clipboard Image Support**: Terminal-aware paste detection (Alt+V for cross-terminal compatibility)
- **Session Persistence**: SQLite-backed session history with resume capability
- **OSC 8 Hyperlinks**: Clickable session links in supported terminals (iTerm2, Ghostty, WezTerm)
- **Honcho Integration**: Optional memory system with session linking
- **Readonly Input During Commands**: Input disabled while spinner animates
- **Bell on Complete**: Optional terminal bell notification (configurable)
- **Resume Display**: Shows abbreviated history when resuming sessions

    57→
    58→def type_text(text: str, speed: float = 0.04, color: str = _CORNSILK):
    59→    """Simulate typing text character by character."""
    60→    for ch in text:
    61→        sys.stdout.write(f"{color}{ch}{_RST}")
    62→        sys.stdout.flush()
    63→        # Variable speed: faster for spaces, slower for punctuation
    64→        if ch in " \t":
    65→            time.sleep(speed * 0.5)
    66→        elif ch in ".,!?;:":
    67→            time.sleep(speed * 2.5)
    68→        else:
    69→            # Add slight jitter
    70→            time.sleep(speed * (0.7 + random.random() * 0.6))
    71→
    72→
    73→def show_prompt(symbol: str = "❯ "):
    74→    """Show the Hermes input prompt."""
    75→    sys.stdout.write(f"\n{_CORNSILK}{symbol}{_RST}")
    76→    sys.stdout.flush()

    82→    sys.stdout.write(f"{_GOLD}● {_BOLD}{text}{_RST}\n")

    86→# ─── Spinner animation ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
    87→
    88→class DemoSpinner:
    89→    """Animated braille spinner with kawaii faces."""
    90→
    91→    def __init__(self):
    92→        self.running = False
    93→        self.thread = None
    94→        self.start_time = 0
    95→        self.frame_idx = 0
    96→        self.message = ""
    97→        self.last_len = 0
    98→
    99→    def start(self, message: str = ""):
   100→        self.message = message
   101→        self.running = True
   102→        self.start_time = time.time()
   103→        self.frame_idx = 0
   104→        self.last_len = 0
   105→        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self._animate, daemon=True)
   106→        self.thread.start()
   107→
   108→    def _animate(self):
   109→        while self.running:
   110→            frame = SPINNER_FRAMES[self.frame_idx % len(SPINNER_FRAMES)]
   111→            elapsed = time.time() - self.start_time
   112→            line = f"  {frame} {self.message} ({elapsed:.1f}s)"
   113→            pad = max(self.last_len - len(line), 0)
   114→            sys.stdout.write(f"\r{line}{' ' * pad}")
   115→            sys.stdout.flush()
   116→            self.last_len = len(line)
   117→            self.frame_idx += 1
   118→            time.sleep(0.12)
   119→
   120→    def update(self, message: str):
   121→        self.message = message
   122→
   123→    def stop(self):
   124→        self.running = False
   125→        if self.thread:
   126→            self.thread.join(timeout=0.5)
   127→        blanks = " " * max(self.last_len + 5, 40)
   128→        sys.stdout.write(f"\r{blanks}\r")
   129→        sys.stdout.flush()
   130→
   131→
   132→# ─── Tool progress lines ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
   133→
   134→def show_tool_line(tool: dict):
   135→    """Print a tool progress line matching Hermes format."""
   136→    icon = tool.get("icon", "⚡")
   137→    verb = tool.get("verb", "tool")
   138→    detail = tool.get("detail", "")
   139→    duration = tool.get("duration", "0.5s")
   140→
   141→    # Pad verb to 9 chars like the real agent
   142→    line = f"  ┊ {icon} {verb:9} {detail}  {duration}"
   143→    sys.stdout.write(f"{_DIM}{line}{_RST}\n")
   144→    sys.stdout.flush()
   145→
   146→
   147→# ─── Response rendering ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
   148→
   149→def show_response(text: str, label: str = " ⚕ Hermes "):
   150→    """Show agent response in a Rich panel matching Hermes styling."""
   151→    console.print(Panel(
   152→        Markdown(text),
   153→        title=f"[bold]{label}[/bold]",
   154→        title_align="left",
   155→        border_style="#CD7F32",
   156→        box=rich_box.HORIZONTALS,
   157→        padding=(1, 2),
   158→    ))
   159→
   160→
   161→# ─── Scene playback ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   162→
   163→def play_scene(scene: dict, typing_speed: float, pause_between: float):
   164→    """Play a single scene (user message → tools → response)."""
   165→
   166→    user_text = scene.get("user", "")
   167→    tools = scene.get("tools", [])
   168→    response = scene.get("response", "")
   169→    thinking_time = scene.get("thinking_time", 2.0)
   170→    response_label = scene.get("response_label", " ⚕ Hermes ")
   171→    pre_pause = scene.get("pre_pause", 0.5)
   172→    post_pause = scene.get("post_pause", 0.5)
   173→
   174→    # --- Show prompt and type user message ---
   175→    if user_text:
   176→        time.sleep(pre_pause)
   177→        show_prompt()
   178→        time.sleep(0.3)
   179→        type_text(user_text, speed=typing_speed)
   180→        time.sleep(0.6)  # Pause before "submit"
   181→        # "Submit" — show as completed message
   182→        show_user_message(user_text)
   183→        time.sleep(0.3)
   184→
   185→    # --- Spinner (thinking) ---
   186→    spinner = DemoSpinner()
   187→    face = random.choice(KAWAII_THINKING)
   188→    verb = random.choice(THINKING_VERBS)
   189→    spinner.start(f"{face} {verb}...")
   190→
   191→    # Simulate thinking time
   192→    # Change verb partway through for realism
   193→    if thinking_time > 2.0:
   194→        time.sleep(thinking_time * 0.4)
   195→        face2 = random.choice(KAWAII_THINKING)
   196→        verb2 = random.choice(THINKING_VERBS)
   197→        spinner.update(f"{face2} {verb2}...")
   198→        time.sleep(thinking_time * 0.6)
   199→    else:
   200→        time.sleep(thinking_time)
   201→
   202→    spinner.stop()
   203→
   204→    # --- Tool progress lines ---
   205→    for tool in tools:
   206→        show_tool_line(tool)
   207→        tool_delay = tool.get("delay", 0.15)
   208→        time.sleep(tool_delay)
   209→
   210→    # --- Agent response ---
   211→    if response:
   212→        show_response(response, label=response_label)
   213→
   214→    time.sleep(post_pause)
   215→
   216→
   217→def play_action(action: dict, typing_speed: float):
   218→    """Play a non-scene action (pause, clear, text, etc.)."""
   219→    action_type = action.get("action")
   220→
   221→    if action_type == "pause":
   222→        time.sleep(action.get("duration", 1.0))
   223→
   224→    elif action_type == "clear":
   225→        os.system("clear")
   226→
   227→    elif action_type == "print":
   228→        text = action.get("text", "")
   229→        color = action.get("color", "")
   230→        if color:
   231→            console.print(f"[{color}]{text}[/]")
   232→        else:
   233→            print(text)
   234→
   235→    elif action_type == "banner":
   236→        # Print a simplified version of the Hermes banner
   237→        _print_demo_banner(action)
   238→
   239→    elif action_type == "type_command":
   240→        # Type a raw command (not in the agent prompt)
   241→        cmd = action.get("command", "")
   242→        prefix = action.get("prefix", "$ ")
   243→        sys.stdout.write(f"\n{_DIM}{prefix}{_RST}")
   244→        sys.stdout.flush()
   245→        time.sleep(0.3)
   246→        type_text(cmd, speed=typing_speed, color=f"{_BOLD}")
   247→        time.sleep(0.5)
   248→        sys.stdout.write("\n")
   249→        sys.stdout.flush()
   250→        output = action.get("output", "")
   251→        if output:
   252→            for line in output.split("\n"):
   253→                sys.stdout.write(f"{_DIM}{line}{_RST}\n")
   254→                sys.stdout.flush()
   255→                time.sleep(0.05)
   256→
   257→

   275→[#CD7F32]⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⢠⣴⠦⠈⠙⠿⣦⡄⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀[/]
   276→[#CD7F32]⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠸⣿⣤⡈⠁⢤⣿⠇⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀[/]

   279→[#B8860B]⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⣿⠁⢰⡆⠈⡿⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀[/]
   280→[#B8860B]⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠈⠳⠈⣡⠞⠁⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀[/]
   281→[#B8860B]⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠈⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀[/]"""

   283→    logo = """[bold #FFD700]██╗  ██╗███████╗██████╗ ███╗   ███╗███████╗███████╗       █████╗  ██████╗ ███████╗███╗   ██╗████████╗[/]

   285→[#FFBF00]███████║█████╗  ██████╔╝██╔████╔██║█████╗  ███████╗█████╗███████║██║  ███╗█████╗  ██╔██╗ ██║   ██║[/]
   286→[#FFBF00]██╔══██║██╔══╝  ██╔══██╗██║╚██╔╝██║██╔══╝  ╚════██║╚════╝██╔══██║██║   ██║██╔══╝  ██║╚██╗██║   ██║[/]

   289→
   290→    from rich.table import Table
   291→
   292→    layout = Table.grid(padding=(0, 2))
   293→    layout.add_column("left", justify="center")
   294→    layout.add_column("right", justify="left")
   295→
   296→    left = f"\n{caduceus}\n\n[#FFBF00]{model}[/] [dim #B8860B]·[/] [dim #B8860B]{context} context[/] [dim #B8860B]·[/] [dim #B8860B]Nous Research[/]\n[dim #B8860B]Session: {session_id}[/]"
   297→
   298→    right_lines = [
   299→        f"[bold #FFBF00]Available Tools[/]",
   300→        f"[dim #B8860B]core_tools:[/] [#FFF8DC]terminal, read_file, write_file, patch, search_files[/]",
   301→        f"[dim #B8860B]web_tools:[/] [#FFF8DC]web_search, web_extract, web_crawl[/]",
   302→        f"[dim #B8860B]browser_tools:[/] [#FFF8DC]browser_navigate, browser_click, browser_type, ...[/]",
   303→        f"[dim #B8860B]agent_tools:[/] [#FFF8DC]delegate_task, mixture_of_agents, memory[/]",
   304→        "",
   305→        f"[bold #FFBF00]Available Skills[/]",
   306→        f"[dim #B8860B]productivity:[/] [#FFF8DC]email-send, web-search, summarize, ...[/]",
   307→        f"[dim #B8860B]development:[/] [#FFF8DC]git-commit, code-review, test-runner, ...[/]",
   308→        "",

   310→    ]
   311→    right = "\n".join(right_lines)
